- **goal-me**: Design a goal as a machine-verifiable closed loop so that /goal can run it without drifting or burning excess cost. Interview the user relentlessly to pin down the spec, steps, per-step goals, and a precise stop condition, write them under .claude/goals/, and produce a ready-to-paste kickoff command. Use when the user says things like "set a goal", "goal me", "prepare for /goal", "design a loop", or "I want to build/research X" (but the requirements are vague).
